# Catalogue

Every variable the collection produces, one per entry. Filter by facet or search in either language: each entry carries both. Unfold one for its inputs, its variants and the card that computes it.

The same collection, for machines: [card.ttl](card.ttl), one SKOS concept per variable. Its URI base is deliberately unresolvable, nothing is published yet.

<details class="cat-row" id="a-FDC" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="mean-flows" data-aspect="magnitude" data-statistic="slope" data-season="record" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.mean-flows.magnitude.slope.record.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">a-FDC</code><code class="v" lang="fr">a-CDC</code><span class="n" lang="en">Slope of the segment between the 33 % and 66 % quantiles of the flow duration curve</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Pente du segment entre les quantiles des débits journaliers à 33 % et 66 % de la courbe des débits classés</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Slope computed on the decimal logarithms of the flows: a steep slope indicates a highly variable regime</span><span lang="fr">Pente calculée sur les logarithmes décimaux des débits : une pente forte signale un régime très variable</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/a-FDC/"><code>a-FDC</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>

<details class="cat-row" id="centerLF_winter" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="timing" data-statistic="threshold-exceedance" data-season="winter" data-output="series" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.low-flows.timing.threshold-exceedance.winter.series.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">centerLF_winter</code><code class="v" lang="fr">centreBE_hiver</code><span class="n" lang="en">Center of winter low flows</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Centre des basses eaux hivernales</span><span class="u">yearday</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">In winter, date of the minimal 10-day mean flow value below the threshold set at the maximum of VCN10 (November to April)</span><span lang="fr">En hiver, date de la valeur minimale des débits moyens sur 10 jours sous le seuil fixé au maximum des VCN10 (mois de novembre à avril)</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#startLF_winter"><code>startLF_winter</code></a> <a href="#endLF_winter"><code>endLF_winter</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/allLF_winter/"><code>allLF_winter</code></a> <a href="../cards/centerLF_winter/"><code>centerLF_winter</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="CR" data-domain="precipitation" data-statistic="ratio" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-purpose="model-performance" data-inputs="R_obs R_sim" data-family="precipitation.ratio.annual.scalar.model-performance.r_obs+r_sim"><summary><code class="v">CR</code><span class="n" lang="en">Correction coefficient for precipitation</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Coefficient correctif des précipitations</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Ratio between the simulated and the observed annual precipitation totals, both averaged over the record</span><span lang="fr">Rapport entre le cumul annuel de précipitations simulé et le cumul observé, tous deux moyennés sur la chronique</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>R_obs</code> <code>R_sim</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/CR/"><code>CR</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="CRS_DJF" data-domain="precipitation" data-statistic="ratio" data-season="by-season" data-output="scalar" data-purpose="model-performance" data-inputs="R_obs R_sim" data-family="precipitation.ratio.by-season.scalar.model-performance.r_obs+r_sim"><summary><code class="v">CRS_DJF</code><span class="n" lang="en">Winter precipitation correction coefficient</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Coefficient correctif des précipitations hivernales</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Ratio between the simulated and the observed precipitation totals over December, January and February, both averaged over the record</span><span lang="fr">Rapport entre le cumul de précipitations simulé et le cumul observé sur les mois de décembre, janvier et février, tous deux moyennés sur la chronique</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>R_obs</code> <code>R_sim</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#CRS_MAM"><code>CRS_MAM</code></a> <a href="#CRS_JJA"><code>CRS_JJA</code></a> <a href="#CRS_SON"><code>CRS_SON</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/CRS_season/"><code>CRS_season</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="CRS_JJA" data-domain="precipitation" data-statistic="ratio" data-season="by-season" data-output="scalar" data-purpose="model-performance" data-inputs="R_obs R_sim" data-family="precipitation.ratio.by-season.scalar.model-performance.r_obs+r_sim"><summary><code class="v">CRS_JJA</code><span class="n" lang="en">Summer precipitation correction coefficient</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Coefficient correctif des précipitations estivales</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Ratio between the simulated and the observed precipitation totals over June, July and August, both averaged over the record</span><span lang="fr">Rapport entre le cumul de précipitations simulé et le cumul observé sur les mois de juin, juillet et août, tous deux moyennés sur la chronique</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>R_obs</code> <code>R_sim</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#CRS_DJF"><code>CRS_DJF</code></a> <a href="#CRS_MAM"><code>CRS_MAM</code></a> <a href="#CRS_SON"><code>CRS_SON</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/CRS_season/"><code>CRS_season</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="CRS_MAM" data-domain="precipitation" data-statistic="ratio" data-season="by-season" data-output="scalar" data-purpose="model-performance" data-inputs="R_obs R_sim" data-family="precipitation.ratio.by-season.scalar.model-performance.r_obs+r_sim"><summary><code class="v">CRS_MAM</code><span class="n" lang="en">Spring precipitation correction coefficient</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Coefficient correctif des précipitations printanières</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Ratio between the simulated and the observed precipitation totals over March, April and May, both averaged over the record</span><span lang="fr">Rapport entre le cumul de précipitations simulé et le cumul observé sur les mois de mars, avril et mai, tous deux moyennés sur la chronique</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>R_obs</code> <code>R_sim</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#CRS_DJF"><code>CRS_DJF</code></a> <a href="#CRS_JJA"><code>CRS_JJA</code></a> <a href="#CRS_SON"><code>CRS_SON</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/CRS_season/"><code>CRS_season</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="CRS_SON" data-domain="precipitation" data-statistic="ratio" data-season="by-season" data-output="scalar" data-purpose="model-performance" data-inputs="R_obs R_sim" data-family="precipitation.ratio.by-season.scalar.model-performance.r_obs+r_sim"><summary><code class="v">CRS_SON</code><span class="n" lang="en">Autumn precipitation correction coefficient</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Coefficient correctif des précipitations automnales</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Ratio between the simulated and the observed precipitation totals over September, October and November, both averaged over the record</span><span lang="fr">Rapport entre le cumul de précipitations simulé et le cumul observé sur les mois de septembre, octobre et novembre, tous deux moyennés sur la chronique</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>R_obs</code> <code>R_sim</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#CRS_DJF"><code>CRS_DJF</code></a> <a href="#CRS_MAM"><code>CRS_MAM</code></a> <a href="#CRS_JJA"><code>CRS_JJA</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/CRS_season/"><code>CRS_season</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="delta-BFI-LH" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="baseflow" data-aspect="magnitude" data-statistic="change" data-season="record" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.baseflow.magnitude.change.record.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v">delta-BFI-LH</code><span class="n" lang="en">Average change of the baseflow index between the target horizon and historical period (Lyne and Hollick)</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Changement moyen de l'indice de débit de base entre l'horizon cible et la période historique (Lyne et Hollick)</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Ratio between mean inter-annual base flow and mean inter-annual flow, the base flow being separated from the hydrograph by the Lyne and Hollick recursive filter</span><span lang="fr">Rapport entre débit de base moyen inter-annuel et débit moyen inter-annuel, le débit de base étant séparé de l'hydrogramme par le filtre récursif de Lyne et Hollick</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#delta-BFI-Wal"><code>delta-BFI-Wal</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/delta-BFI-LH_H/"><code>delta-BFI-LH_H</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="delta-BFI-Wal" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="baseflow" data-aspect="magnitude" data-statistic="change" data-season="record" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.baseflow.magnitude.change.record.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v">delta-BFI-Wal</code><span class="n" lang="en">Average change of the baseflow index between the target horizon and historical period (Wallingford)</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Changement moyen de l'indice de débit de base entre l'horizon cible et la période historique (Wallingford)</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Ratio between mean inter-annual base flow and mean inter-annual flow, the base flow being separated from the hydrograph by the smoothed minima method (Wallingford)</span><span lang="fr">Rapport entre débit de base moyen inter-annuel et débit moyen inter-annuel, le débit de base étant séparé de l'hydrogramme par la méthode des minima lissés (Wallingford)</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#delta-BFI-LH"><code>delta-BFI-LH</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/delta-BFI-Wal_H/"><code>delta-BFI-Wal_H</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="delta-centerBF" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="baseflow" data-aspect="timing" data-statistic="change" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.baseflow.timing.change.annual.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">delta-centerBF</code><code class="v" lang="fr">delta-centreQB</code><span class="n" lang="en">Average change of the center of low flows between the target horizon and historical period</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Changement moyen du centre des écoulements lents entre l'horizon cible et la période historique</span><span class="u">day</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Date when 50 % of the annual cumulative baseflow is reached</span><span lang="fr">Date à laquelle 50 % du cumul annuel du débit de base sont atteints</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#delta-endBF"><code>delta-endBF</code></a> <a href="#delta-startBF"><code>delta-startBF</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/delta-centerBF_H/"><code>delta-centerBF_H</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="delta-centerLF" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="timing" data-statistic="change" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.low-flows.timing.change.annual.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">delta-centerLF</code><code class="v" lang="fr">delta-centreBE</code><span class="n" lang="en">Average change of the center of low flows between the target horizon and historical period</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Changement moyen du centre des basses eaux entre l'horizon cible et la période historique</span><span class="u">day</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Date of the minimal 10-day mean flow value below the threshold set at the maximum of VCN10</span><span lang="fr">Date de la valeur minimale des débits moyens sur 10 jours sous le seuil fixé au maximum des VCN10</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#delta-startLF"><code>delta-startLF</code></a> <a href="#delta-endLF"><code>delta-endLF</code></a> <a href="#delta-tVCN10"><code>delta-tVCN10</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/delta-allLF_H/"><code>delta-allLF_H</code></a> <a href="../cards/delta-centerLF_H/"><code>delta-centerLF_H</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="delta-centerLF_summer" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="timing" data-statistic="change" data-season="summer" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.low-flows.timing.change.summer.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">delta-centerLF_summer</code><code class="v" lang="fr">delta-centreBE_été</code><span class="n" lang="en">Average change of the center of summer low flows between the target horizon and historical period</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Changement moyen du centre des basses eaux estivales entre l'horizon cible et la période historique</span><span class="u">day</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">In summer, date of the minimal 10-day mean flow value below the threshold set at the maximum of VCN10 (May to November)</span><span lang="fr">En été, date de la valeur minimale des débits moyens sur 10 jours sous le seuil fixé au maximum des VCN10 (mois de mai à novembre)</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#delta-startLF_summer"><code>delta-startLF_summer</code></a> <a href="#delta-endLF_summer"><code>delta-endLF_summer</code></a> <a href="#delta-tVCN10_summer"><code>delta-tVCN10_summer</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/delta-allLF_summer_H/"><code>delta-allLF_summer_H</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="delta-centerLF_winter" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="timing" data-statistic="change" data-season="winter" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.low-flows.timing.change.winter.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">delta-centerLF_winter</code><code class="v" lang="fr">delta-centreBE_hiver</code><span class="n" lang="en">Average change of the center of winter low flows between the target horizon and historical period</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Changement moyen du centre des basses eaux hivernales entre l'horizon cible et la période historique</span><span class="u">day</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">In winter, date of the minimal 10-day mean flow value below the threshold set at the maximum of VCN10 (November to April)</span><span lang="fr">En hiver, date de la valeur minimale des débits moyens sur 10 jours sous le seuil fixé au maximum des VCN10 (mois de novembre à avril)</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#delta-startLF_winter"><code>delta-startLF_winter</code></a> <a href="#delta-endLF_winter"><code>delta-endLF_winter</code></a> <a href="#delta-tVCN10_winter"><code>delta-tVCN10_winter</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/delta-allLF_winter_H/"><code>delta-allLF_winter_H</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="delta-dtBF" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="baseflow" data-aspect="duration" data-statistic="change" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.baseflow.duration.change.annual.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">delta-dtBF</code><code class="v" lang="fr">delta-dtQB</code><span class="n" lang="en">Average change of the duration of low flows between the target horizon and historical period</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Changement moyen de la durée des écoulements lents entre l'horizon cible et la période historique</span><span class="u">day</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Duration between the start and end of low flows</span><span lang="fr">Durée entre le début et la fin des écoulements lents</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/delta-dtBF_H/"><code>delta-dtBF_H</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="delta-dtFlood" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="high-flows" data-aspect="duration" data-statistic="change" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.high-flows.duration.change.annual.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">delta-dtFlood</code><code class="v" lang="fr">delta-dtCrue</code><span class="n" lang="en">Average change of the duration of floods between the target horizon and historical period</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Changement moyen de la durée des crues entre l'horizon cible et la période historique</span><span class="u">day</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Duration of floods, sampled by annual maxima. The series exploited is the difference between the raw flow and the base flow, assimilated to runoff Qr. Floods are identified based on the annual maximum in the Qr series, and for each flood, the duration is defined as the number of days where Qr is above half of the annual maxima.</span><span lang="fr">Durée des crues, échantillonnées par maxima annuel. La série exploitée est la différence entre le débit brut et le débit de base, assimilé au ruissellement Qr. Les crues sont identifiées sur la base du maximum annuel dans la série des Qr et pour chaque crue, la durée est définie comme le nombre de jours où Qr est supérieur au maxima annuel divisé par 2.</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/delta-dtFlood_H/"><code>delta-dtFlood_H</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="delta-dtLF" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="duration" data-statistic="change" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.low-flows.duration.change.annual.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">delta-dtLF</code><code class="v" lang="fr">delta-dtBE</code><span class="n" lang="en">Average change of duration of low flows between the target horizon and historical period</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Changement moyen de la durée des basses eaux entre l'horizon cible et la période historique</span><span class="u">day</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Duration of the longest continuous sequence with 10-day mean flows below the threshold set at the maximum of VCN10</span><span lang="fr">Durée de la plus longue séquence continue avec des débits moyens sur 10 jours sous le seuil fixé au maximum des VCN10</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/delta-allLF_H/"><code>delta-allLF_H</code></a> <a href="../cards/delta-dtLF_H/"><code>delta-dtLF_H</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="delta-dtLF_summer" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="duration" data-statistic="change" data-season="summer" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.low-flows.duration.change.summer.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">delta-dtLF_summer</code><code class="v" lang="fr">delta-dtBE_été</code><span class="n" lang="en">Average change of duration of summer low flows between the target horizon and historical period</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Changement moyen de la durée des basses eaux estivales entre l'horizon cible et la période historique</span><span class="u">day</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">In summer, duration of the longest continuous sequence with 10-day mean flows below the threshold set at the maximum of VCN10 (May to November)</span><span lang="fr">En été, durée de la plus longue séquence continue avec des débits moyens sur 10 jours sous le seuil fixé au maximum des VCN10 (mois de mai à novembre)</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/delta-allLF_summer_H/"><code>delta-allLF_summer_H</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="delta-dtLF_winter" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="duration" data-statistic="change" data-season="winter" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.low-flows.duration.change.winter.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">delta-dtLF_winter</code><code class="v" lang="fr">delta-dtBE_hiver</code><span class="n" lang="en">Average change of duration of winter low flows between the target horizon and historical period</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Changement moyen de la durée des basses eaux hivernales entre l'horizon cible et la période historique</span><span class="u">day</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">In winter, duration of the longest continuous sequence with 10-day mean flows below the threshold set at the maximum of VCN10 (November to April)</span><span lang="fr">En hiver, durée de la plus longue séquence continue avec des débits moyens sur 10 jours sous le seuil fixé au maximum des VCN10 (mois de novembre à avril)</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/delta-allLF_winter_H/"><code>delta-allLF_winter_H</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="delta-endBF" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="baseflow" data-aspect="timing" data-statistic="change" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.baseflow.timing.change.annual.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">delta-endBF</code><code class="v" lang="fr">delta-finQB</code><span class="n" lang="en">Average change of the end of Base Flow between the target horizon and historical period</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Changement moyen de la fin des écoulements lents entre l'horizon cible et la période historique</span><span class="u">day</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Date when 90 % of the annual cumulative base flow is reached</span><span lang="fr">Date à laquelle 90 % du cumul annuel du débit de base sont atteints</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#delta-centerBF"><code>delta-centerBF</code></a> <a href="#delta-startBF"><code>delta-startBF</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/delta-endBF_H/"><code>delta-endBF_H</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="delta-endLF" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="timing" data-statistic="change" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.low-flows.timing.change.annual.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">delta-endLF</code><code class="v" lang="fr">delta-finBE</code><span class="n" lang="en">Average change of the end of low flows between the target horizon and historical period</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Changement moyen de la fin des basses eaux entre l'horizon cible et la période historique</span><span class="u">day</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Date of the last 10-day mean flow value below the threshold set at the maximum of VCN10</span><span lang="fr">Date de la dernière valeur de débits moyens sur 10 jours sous le seuil fixé au maximum des VCN10</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#delta-startLF"><code>delta-startLF</code></a> <a href="#delta-centerLF"><code>delta-centerLF</code></a> <a href="#delta-tVCN10"><code>delta-tVCN10</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/delta-allLF_H/"><code>delta-allLF_H</code></a> <a href="../cards/delta-endLF_H/"><code>delta-endLF_H</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>

<details class="cat-row" id="delta-vLF_summer" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="magnitude" data-statistic="change" data-season="summer" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.low-flows.magnitude.change.summer.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">delta-vLF_summer</code><code class="v" lang="fr">delta-vBE_été</code><span class="n" lang="en">Average change of the deficit volume of summer low flows between the target horizon and historical period</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Changement moyen du volume de déficit des basses eaux estivales entre l'horizon cible et la période historique</span><span class="u">%</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">In summer, sum of the differences between the 10-day mean and the maximum of VCN10, over the longest sequence below this threshold (May to November)</span><span lang="fr">En été, somme des écarts entre la moyenne sur 10 jours et le maximum des VCN10, sur la séquence la plus longue sous ce seuil (mois de mai à novembre)</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#delta-QMNA_summer"><code>delta-QMNA_summer</code></a> <a href="#delta-QNA_summer"><code>delta-QNA_summer</code></a> <a href="#delta-VCN10_summer"><code>delta-VCN10_summer</code></a> <a href="#delta-VCN30_summer"><code>delta-VCN30_summer</code></a> <a href="#delta-VCN3_summer"><code>delta-VCN3_summer</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/delta-allLF_summer_H/"><code>delta-allLF_summer_H</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>

<details class="cat-row" id="dtFlood" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="high-flows" data-aspect="duration" data-statistic="threshold-exceedance" data-season="annual" data-output="series" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.high-flows.duration.threshold-exceedance.annual.series.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">dtFlood</code><code class="v" lang="fr">dtCrue</code><span class="n" lang="en">Duration of floods</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Durée des crues</span><span class="u">day</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Duration of floods, sampled by annual maxima. The series exploited is the difference between the raw flow and the base flow, assimilated to runoff Qr. Floods are identified based on the annual maximum in the Qr series, and for each flood, the duration is defined as the number of days where Qr is above half of the annual maxima.</span><span lang="fr">Durée des crues, échantillonnées par maxima annuel. La série exploitée est la différence entre le débit brut et le débit de base, assimilé au ruissellement Qr. Les crues sont identifiées sur la base du maximum annuel dans la série des Qr et pour chaque crue, la durée est définie comme le nombre de jours où Qr est supérieur au maxima annuel divisé par 2.</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/dtFlood/"><code>dtFlood</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="dtLF" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="duration" data-statistic="threshold-exceedance" data-season="annual" data-output="series" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.low-flows.duration.threshold-exceedance.annual.series.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">dtLF</code><code class="v" lang="fr">dtBE</code><span class="n" lang="en">Duration of low flows</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Durée des basses eaux</span><span class="u">day</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Duration of the longest continuous sequence with 10-day mean flows below the threshold set at the maximum of VCN10</span><span lang="fr">Durée de la plus longue séquence continue avec des débits moyens sur 10 jours sous le seuil fixé au maximum des VCN10</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/allLF/"><code>allLF</code></a> <a href="../cards/dtLF/"><code>dtLF</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="dtLF_summer" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="duration" data-statistic="threshold-exceedance" data-season="summer" data-output="series" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.low-flows.duration.threshold-exceedance.summer.series.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">dtLF_summer</code><code class="v" lang="fr">dtBE_été</code><span class="n" lang="en">Duration of summer low flows</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Durée des basses eaux estivales</span><span class="u">day</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">In summer, duration of the longest continuous sequence with 10-day mean flows below the threshold set at the maximum of VCN10 (May to November)</span><span lang="fr">En été, durée de la plus longue séquence continue avec des débits moyens sur 10 jours sous le seuil fixé au maximum des VCN10 (mois de mai à novembre)</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/allLF_summer/"><code>allLF_summer</code></a> <a href="../cards/dtLF_summer/"><code>dtLF_summer</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>

<details class="cat-row" id="FDC_p" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="mean-flows" data-aspect="magnitude" data-statistic="quantile" data-season="record" data-output="curve" data-inputs="Q period_start? period_end?" data-family="flow.mean-flows.magnitude.quantile.record.curve.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">FDC_p</code><code class="v" lang="fr">CDC_p</code><span class="n" lang="en">Flow duration curve over the whole record</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Courbe des débits classés sur la chronique entière</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Curve of flow quantiles as a function of exceedance probability; the card outputs its two coordinates (FDC_p, unitless probabilities; FDC_Q, flow quantiles)</span><span lang="fr">Courbe des quantiles de débit en fonction de la probabilité de dépassement ; la fiche produit ses deux coordonnées (FDC_p, probabilités sans unité ; FDC_Q, quantiles de débit)</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code> <code>period_start?</code> <code>period_end?</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#FDC_Q"><code>FDC_Q</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/FDC/"><code>FDC</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="FDC_Q" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="mean-flows" data-aspect="magnitude" data-statistic="quantile" data-season="record" data-output="curve" data-inputs="Q period_start? period_end?" data-family="flow.mean-flows.magnitude.quantile.record.curve.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">FDC_Q</code><code class="v" lang="fr">CDC_Q</code><span class="n" lang="en">Flow duration curve over the whole record</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Courbe des débits classés sur la chronique entière</span><span class="u">m³·s⁻¹</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Curve of flow quantiles as a function of exceedance probability; the card outputs its two coordinates (FDC_p, unitless probabilities; FDC_Q, flow quantiles)</span><span lang="fr">Courbe des quantiles de débit en fonction de la probabilité de dépassement ; la fiche produit ses deux coordonnées (FDC_p, probabilités sans unité ; FDC_Q, quantiles de débit)</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code> <code>period_start?</code> <code>period_end?</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#FDC_p"><code>FDC_p</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/FDC/"><code>FDC</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>

<details class="cat-row" id="KGE" data-domain="flow" data-statistic="efficiency" data-season="record" data-output="scalar" data-purpose="model-performance" data-inputs="Q_obs Q_sim" data-family="flow.efficiency.record.scalar.model-performance.q_obs+q_sim"><summary><code class="v">KGE</code><span class="n" lang="en">Kling-Gupta Performance Coefficient</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Coefficient de performance de Kling-Gupta</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">It measures the proximity between reference and simulated data series based on three sub-criteria (r, alpha, and beta) with equal weights. The coefficient gives strong weight to the reconstruction of high values of the examined variable.</span><span lang="fr">Il mesure la proximité entre les séries de données de référence et celles simulées, sur la base de trois sous-critères (r, alpha et beta) aux pondérations identiques. Le coefficient donne un poids fort à la reconstitution des valeurs fortes de la variable examinée.</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q_obs</code> <code>Q_sim</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#KGEsqrt"><code>KGEsqrt</code></a> <a href="#NSE"><code>NSE</code></a> <a href="#NSEinv"><code>NSEinv</code></a> <a href="#NSElog"><code>NSElog</code></a> <a href="#NSEsqrt"><code>NSEsqrt</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/KGE/"><code>KGE</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="KGEsqrt" data-domain="flow" data-statistic="efficiency" data-season="record" data-output="scalar" data-purpose="model-performance" data-inputs="Q_obs Q_sim" data-family="flow.efficiency.record.scalar.model-performance.q_obs+q_sim"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">KGEsqrt</code><code class="v" lang="fr">KGEracine</code><span class="n" lang="en">Kling-Gupta Efficiency of the square root of data</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Coefficient d'efficience de Kling-Gupta de la racine carrée des données</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Similar to KGE, this score gives equivalent weight across the entire range of the evaluated variable.</span><span lang="fr">Identique au KGE, ce score donne cependant un poids équivalent sur l'ensemble de la plage de variation de la variable évaluée.</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q_obs</code> <code>Q_sim</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#KGE"><code>KGE</code></a> <a href="#NSE"><code>NSE</code></a> <a href="#NSEinv"><code>NSEinv</code></a> <a href="#NSElog"><code>NSElog</code></a> <a href="#NSEsqrt"><code>NSEsqrt</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/KGEsqrt/"><code>KGEsqrt</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>

<details class="cat-row" id="median-centerBF" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="baseflow" data-aspect="timing" data-statistic="median" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.baseflow.timing.median.annual.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">median-centerBF</code><code class="v" lang="fr">mediane-centreQB</code><span class="n" lang="en">Inter-annual median of the center of baseflow</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Médiane inter-annuelle du centre des écoulements lents</span><span class="u">yearday</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Median of the dates at which 50 % of the annual cumulative baseflow is reached</span><span lang="fr">Médiane des dates à laquelle 50 % du cumul annuel du débit de base sont atteints</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#median-endBF"><code>median-endBF</code></a> <a href="#median-startBF"><code>median-startBF</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/median-centerBF/"><code>median-centerBF</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="median-centerLF" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="timing" data-statistic="median" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.low-flows.timing.median.annual.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">median-centerLF</code><code class="v" lang="fr">mediane-centreBE</code><span class="n" lang="en">Inter-annual median of the center of low flows</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Médiane inter-annuelle du centre des basses eaux</span><span class="u">yearday</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Median of the dates of the minimal 10-day mean flow value below the threshold set at the maximum of VCN10</span><span lang="fr">Médiane des dates de la valeur minimale des débits moyens sur 10 jours sous le seuil fixé au maximum des VCN10</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#median-startLF"><code>median-startLF</code></a> <a href="#median-endLF"><code>median-endLF</code></a> <a href="#median-tVCN10"><code>median-tVCN10</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/median-allLF/"><code>median-allLF</code></a> <a href="../cards/median-centerLF/"><code>median-centerLF</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>

<details class="cat-row" id="median-startLF" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="timing" data-statistic="median" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.low-flows.timing.median.annual.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">median-startLF</code><code class="v" lang="fr">mediane-debutBE</code><span class="n" lang="en">Inter-annual median of the start of low flows</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Médiane inter-annuelle du début des basses eaux</span><span class="u">yearday</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Median of the dates of the first 10-day mean flow value below the threshold set at the maximum of VCN10</span><span lang="fr">Médiane des dates de la première valeur de débits moyens sur 10 jours sous le seuil fixé au maximum des VCN10</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#median-centerLF"><code>median-centerLF</code></a> <a href="#median-endLF"><code>median-endLF</code></a> <a href="#median-tVCN10"><code>median-tVCN10</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/median-allLF/"><code>median-allLF</code></a> <a href="../cards/median-startLF/"><code>median-startLF</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>

<details class="cat-row" id="median-tVCX10" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="high-flows" data-aspect="timing" data-statistic="median" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.high-flows.timing.median.annual.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">median-tVCX10</code><code class="v" lang="fr">mediane-tVCX10</code><span class="n" lang="en">Inter-annual median of the dates of the annual maximum of 10-day mean flows</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Médiane inter-annuelle des dates du maximum annuel des débits moyens sur 10 jours</span><span class="u">yearday</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#median-tQJXA"><code>median-tQJXA</code></a> <a href="#median-tVCX3"><code>median-tVCX3</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/median-tVCX10/"><code>median-tVCX10</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="median-tVCX3" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="high-flows" data-aspect="timing" data-statistic="median" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.high-flows.timing.median.annual.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">median-tVCX3</code><code class="v" lang="fr">mediane-tVCX3</code><span class="n" lang="en">Inter-annual median of the dates of the annual maximum of 3-day mean flows</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Médiane inter-annuelle des dates du maximum annuel des débits moyens sur 3 jours</span><span class="u">yearday</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#median-tQJXA"><code>median-tQJXA</code></a> <a href="#median-tVCX10"><code>median-tVCX10</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/median-tVCX3/"><code>median-tVCX3</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="median-vBF" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="baseflow" data-aspect="magnitude" data-statistic="median" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.baseflow.magnitude.median.annual.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">median-vBF</code><code class="v" lang="fr">mediane-vQB</code><span class="n" lang="en">Inter-annual median of the annual volume generated by base flow</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Médiane inter-annuelle du volume annuel généré par les écoulements lents</span><span class="u">hm³</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Sum of the daily volumes of base flow, separated from the hydrograph by the smoothed minima method (Wallingford)</span><span lang="fr">Somme des volumes journaliers du débit de base, séparé de l'hydrogramme par la méthode des minima lissés (Wallingford)</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/median-vBF/"><code>median-vBF</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="median-vLF" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="magnitude" data-statistic="median" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.low-flows.magnitude.median.annual.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">median-vLF</code><code class="v" lang="fr">mediane-vBE</code><span class="n" lang="en">Inter-annual median of the deficit volume of low flows</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Médiane inter-annuelle des volumes de déficit des basses eaux</span><span class="u">hm³</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Median of the sums of the differences between the 10-day mean and the maximum of VCN10, over the longest sequence below this threshold</span><span lang="fr">Médiane des sommes des écarts entre la moyenne sur 10 jours et le maximum des VCN10, sur la séquence la plus longue sous ce seuil</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/median-allLF/"><code>median-allLF</code></a> <a href="../cards/median-vLF/"><code>median-vLF</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="n-QJXA-10" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="high-flows" data-aspect="frequency" data-statistic="threshold-exceedance" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.high-flows.frequency.threshold-exceedance.annual.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v">n-QJXA-10</code><span class="n" lang="en">Number of years in the target horizon where QJXA is superior to QJXA-10 from the historical period</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Nombre d'années de l'horizon cible où le QJXA est supérieur au QJXA-10 de la période historique</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/n-QJXA-10_H/"><code>n-QJXA-10_H</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="n-VCN10-5" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="frequency" data-statistic="threshold-exceedance" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q" data-family="flow.low-flows.frequency.threshold-exceedance.annual.scalar.q"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">n-VCN10-5</code><code class="v" lang="fr">nVCN10-5</code><span class="n" lang="en">Number of years in the target horizon where VCN10 is below or equal to VCN10-5 from the historical period</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Nombre d'années de l'horizon cible où le VCN10 est inférieur ou égal au VCN10-5 de la période historique</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/n-VCN10-5_H/"><code>n-VCN10-5_H</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="NSE" data-domain="flow" data-statistic="efficiency" data-season="record" data-output="scalar" data-purpose="model-performance" data-inputs="Q_obs Q_sim" data-family="flow.efficiency.record.scalar.model-performance.q_obs+q_sim"><summary><code class="v">NSE</code><span class="n" lang="en">Nash-Sutcliffe Efficiency</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Coefficient d'efficience de Nash-Sutcliffe</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">It measures the proximity between the reference data series and the simulated ones, based on the square deviation. The coefficient gives strong emphasis on the reconstruction of high values of the examined variable.</span><span lang="fr">Il mesure la proximité entre les séries de données de référence et celles simulées, sur la base de l'écart quadratique. Le coefficient donne un poids fort à la reconstitution des valeurs fortes de la variable examinée.</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q_obs</code> <code>Q_sim</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#KGE"><code>KGE</code></a> <a href="#KGEsqrt"><code>KGEsqrt</code></a> <a href="#NSEinv"><code>NSEinv</code></a> <a href="#NSElog"><code>NSElog</code></a> <a href="#NSEsqrt"><code>NSEsqrt</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/NSE/"><code>NSE</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="NSEinv" data-domain="flow" data-statistic="efficiency" data-season="record" data-output="scalar" data-purpose="model-performance" data-inputs="Q_obs Q_sim" data-family="flow.efficiency.record.scalar.model-performance.q_obs+q_sim"><summary><code class="v">NSEinv</code><span class="n" lang="en">Nash-Sutcliffe Efficiency of the inverse of the data</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Coefficient d'efficience de Nash-Sutcliffe de l'inverse des données</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Identical to NSE, this score, however, gives strong emphasis on the reconstruction of low values of the examined variable.</span><span lang="fr">Identique au NSE, ce score donne cependant un poids fort à la reconstitution des valeurs faibles de la variable examinée.</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q_obs</code> <code>Q_sim</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#KGE"><code>KGE</code></a> <a href="#KGEsqrt"><code>KGEsqrt</code></a> <a href="#NSE"><code>NSE</code></a> <a href="#NSElog"><code>NSElog</code></a> <a href="#NSEsqrt"><code>NSEsqrt</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/NSEinv/"><code>NSEinv</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="NSElog" data-domain="flow" data-statistic="efficiency" data-season="record" data-output="scalar" data-purpose="model-performance" data-inputs="Q_obs Q_sim" data-family="flow.efficiency.record.scalar.model-performance.q_obs+q_sim"><summary><code class="v">NSElog</code><span class="n" lang="en">Nash-Sutcliffe Efficiency of the logarithm of the data</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Coefficient d'efficience de Nash-Sutcliffe du logarithme des données</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Identical to NSE, this score, however, gives strong emphasis on the reconstruction of low values of the examined variable.</span><span lang="fr">Identique au NSE, ce score donne cependant un poids fort à la reconstitution des valeurs faibles de la variable examinée.</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q_obs</code> <code>Q_sim</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#KGE"><code>KGE</code></a> <a href="#KGEsqrt"><code>KGEsqrt</code></a> <a href="#NSE"><code>NSE</code></a> <a href="#NSEinv"><code>NSEinv</code></a> <a href="#NSEsqrt"><code>NSEsqrt</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/NSElog/"><code>NSElog</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="NSEsqrt" data-domain="flow" data-statistic="efficiency" data-season="record" data-output="scalar" data-purpose="model-performance" data-inputs="Q_obs Q_sim" data-family="flow.efficiency.record.scalar.model-performance.q_obs+q_sim"><summary><code class="v" lang="en">NSEsqrt</code><code class="v" lang="fr">NSEracine</code><span class="n" lang="en">Nash-Sutcliffe Efficiency of the square root of the data</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Coefficient d'efficience de Nash-Sutcliffe de la racine carrée des données</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Identical to NSE, this score, however, gives equal weight across the entire range of the evaluated variable.</span><span lang="fr">Identique au NSE, ce score donne cependant un poids équivalent sur l'ensemble de la plage de variation de la variable évaluée.</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q_obs</code> <code>Q_sim</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#KGE"><code>KGE</code></a> <a href="#KGEsqrt"><code>KGEsqrt</code></a> <a href="#NSE"><code>NSE</code></a> <a href="#NSEinv"><code>NSEinv</code></a> <a href="#NSElog"><code>NSElog</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/NSEsqrt/"><code>NSEsqrt</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>

<details class="cat-row" id="QR_ratio" data-domain="flow precipitation" data-statistic="ratio" data-season="record" data-output="scalar" data-purpose="climate-sensitivity" data-inputs="Q R" data-family="flow.precipitation.ratio.record.scalar.climate-sensitivity.q+r"><summary><code class="v">QR_ratio</code><span class="n" lang="en">Ratio of cumulative flow to cumulative precipitation</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Rapport des cumuls débit sur précipitations</span><span class="u">m³·s⁻¹·mm⁻¹</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Ratio between the sum of flows and the sum of precipitation. Proportional to the runoff coefficient through the basin area (dimensionless coefficient = 86.4 × ratio / area in km²): suited to temporal monitoring of a station, not to comparison between basins</span><span lang="fr">Rapport entre la somme des débits et la somme des précipitations. Proportionnel au coefficient de ruissellement via la surface du bassin (coefficient adimensionnel = 86,4 × rapport / surface en km²) : adapté au suivi temporel d'une station, pas à la comparaison entre bassins</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code> <code>R</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/QR_ratio/"><code>QR_ratio</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>

<details class="cat-row" id="RAT_ET0" data-domain="flow evapotranspiration" data-statistic="correlation" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-purpose="climate-sensitivity" data-inputs="Q_obs Q_sim ET0_obs" data-family="flow.evapotranspiration.correlation.annual.scalar.climate-sensitivity.et0_obs+q_obs+q_sim"><summary><code class="v">RAT_ET0</code><span class="n" lang="en">Robustness test to a variation in reference evapotranspiration</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Test de robustesse à une variation d'évapotranspiration de référence</span><span class="u">bool</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">True when the model bias is correlated with the reference evapotranspiration, in the sense of a Spearman correlation significant at the 5 % level: performance then depends on that variable, so the model does not behave the same everywhere</span><span lang="fr">Vrai quand le biais du modèle est corrélé à l'évapotranspiration de référence, au sens d'une corrélation de Spearman significative au seuil de 5 % : la performance dépend alors de cette variable, donc le modèle ne se comporte pas de la même façon partout</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q_obs</code> <code>Q_sim</code> <code>ET0_obs</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/RAT_ET0/"><code>RAT_ET0</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="RAT_R" data-domain="flow precipitation" data-statistic="correlation" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-purpose="climate-sensitivity" data-inputs="Q_obs Q_sim R_obs" data-family="flow.precipitation.correlation.annual.scalar.climate-sensitivity.q_obs+q_sim+r_obs"><summary><code class="v">RAT_R</code><span class="n" lang="en">Robustness test to a variation in precipitation</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Test de robustesse à une variation de précipitations</span><span class="u">bool</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">True when the model bias is correlated with precipitation, in the sense of a Spearman correlation significant at the 5 % level: performance then depends on that variable, so the model does not behave the same everywhere</span><span lang="fr">Vrai quand le biais du modèle est corrélé aux précipitations, au sens d'une corrélation de Spearman significative au seuil de 5 % : la performance dépend alors de cette variable, donc le modèle ne se comporte pas de la même façon partout</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q_obs</code> <code>Q_sim</code> <code>R_obs</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/RAT_R/"><code>RAT_R</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="RAT_T" data-domain="flow temperature" data-statistic="correlation" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-purpose="climate-sensitivity" data-inputs="Q_obs Q_sim T_obs" data-family="flow.temperature.correlation.annual.scalar.climate-sensitivity.q_obs+q_sim+t_obs"><summary><code class="v">RAT_T</code><span class="n" lang="en">Robustness test to air temperature variation</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Test de robustesse à une variation de température de l'air</span><span class="u">bool</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">True when the model bias is correlated with air temperature, in the sense of a Spearman correlation significant at the 5 % level: performance then depends on that variable, so the model does not behave the same everywhere</span><span lang="fr">Vrai quand le biais du modèle est corrélé à la température de l'air, au sens d'une corrélation de Spearman significative au seuil de 5 % : la performance dépend alors de cette variable, donc le modèle ne se comporte pas de la même façon partout</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q_obs</code> <code>Q_sim</code> <code>T_obs</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/RAT_T/"><code>RAT_T</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="RCXA1" data-domain="precipitation" data-phenomenon="heavy-rain" data-aspect="magnitude" data-statistic="maximum" data-season="annual" data-output="series" data-inputs="R" data-family="precipitation.heavy-rain.magnitude.maximum.annual.series.r"><summary><code class="v">RCXA1</code><span class="n" lang="en">Annual maximum of daily precipitation</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Maximum annuel des précipitations journalières</span><span class="u">mm</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>R</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#RCXA5"><code>RCXA5</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/RCXA1/"><code>RCXA1</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>

<details class="cat-row" id="Rl_ratio" data-domain="precipitation" data-phenomenon="snow" data-aspect="magnitude" data-statistic="ratio" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="R Rl Rs" data-family="precipitation.snow.magnitude.ratio.annual.scalar.r+rl+rs"><summary><code class="v">Rl_ratio</code><span class="n" lang="en">Ratio of liquid to total precipitation</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Rapport des précipitations liquides aux précipitations totales</span><span class="u">without unit</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>R</code> <code>Rl</code> <code>Rs</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#Rs_ratio"><code>Rs_ratio</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/RA_ratio/"><code>RA_ratio</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>

<details class="cat-row" id="rp-QMNA" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="frequency" data-statistic="return-period" data-season="by-month" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q Q_lim" data-family="flow.low-flows.frequency.return-period.by-month.scalar.q+q_lim"><summary><code class="v">rp-QMNA</code><span class="n" lang="en">Return period of the station's regulatory threshold discharge in the distribution of the annual minima of monthly flows</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Période de retour du débit seuil réglementaire de la station dans la distribution des minimums annuels des débits mensuels</span><span class="u">year</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Inverse of the return-level fiches (QMNA-5) - the log-normal law is fitted on the QMNA series and evaluated at the constant threshold discharge Q_lim given by regulatory texts (e.g. DOE, DCR); a threshold far below the fitted law gives an extrapolated return period, reported as is</span><span lang="fr">Inverse des fiches de niveau de retour (QMNA-5) - la loi log-normale est ajustée sur la série des QMNA puis évaluée au débit seuil constant Q_lim donné par les textes réglementaires (par exemple DOE, DCR) ; un seuil loin du corps de la loi donne une période de retour extrapolée, restituée telle quelle</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code> <code>Q_lim</code></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/rp-QMNA/"><code>rp-QMNA</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="rp-VCN10" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="frequency" data-statistic="return-period" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q Q_lim" data-family="flow.low-flows.frequency.return-period.annual.scalar.q+q_lim"><summary><code class="v">rp-VCN10</code><span class="n" lang="en">Return period of the station's regulatory threshold discharge in the distribution of the annual minima of 10-day mean flows</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Période de retour du débit seuil réglementaire de la station dans la distribution des minimums annuels des débits moyens sur 10 jours</span><span class="u">year</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Inverse of the return-level fiches (VCN10-5) - the log-normal law is fitted on the VCN10 series and evaluated at the constant threshold discharge Q_lim given by regulatory texts (e.g. DOE, DCR); a threshold far below the fitted law gives an extrapolated return period, reported as is</span><span lang="fr">Inverse des fiches de niveau de retour (VCN10-5) - la loi log-normale est ajustée sur la série des VCN10 puis évaluée au débit seuil constant Q_lim donné par les textes réglementaires (par exemple DOE, DCR) ; un seuil loin du corps de la loi donne une période de retour extrapolée, restituée telle quelle</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code> <code>Q_lim</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#rp-VCN30"><code>rp-VCN30</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/rp-VCN10/"><code>rp-VCN10</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
<details class="cat-row" id="rp-VCN30" data-domain="flow" data-phenomenon="low-flows" data-aspect="frequency" data-statistic="return-period" data-season="annual" data-output="scalar" data-inputs="Q Q_lim" data-family="flow.low-flows.frequency.return-period.annual.scalar.q+q_lim"><summary><code class="v">rp-VCN30</code><span class="n" lang="en">Return period of the station's regulatory threshold discharge in the distribution of the annual minima of 30-day mean flows</span><span class="n" lang="fr">Période de retour du débit seuil réglementaire de la station dans la distribution des minimums annuels des débits moyens sur 30 jours</span><span class="u">year</span></summary><div class="cat-detail"><p class="cat-desc"><span lang="en">Inverse of the return-level fiches (VCN30-2) - the log-normal law is fitted on the VCN30 series and evaluated at the constant threshold discharge Q_lim given by regulatory texts (e.g. DOE, DCR); a threshold far below the fitted law gives an extrapolated return period, reported as is</span><span lang="fr">Inverse des fiches de niveau de retour (VCN30-2) - la loi log-normale est ajustée sur la série des VCN30 puis évaluée au débit seuil constant Q_lim donné par les textes réglementaires (par exemple DOE, DCR) ; un seuil loin du corps de la loi donne une période de retour extrapolée, restituée telle quelle</span></p><dl><dt>inputs</dt><dd><code>Q</code> <code>Q_lim</code></dd><dt>variants</dt><dd><a href="#rp-VCN10"><code>rp-VCN10</code></a></dd><dt>card</dt><dd><a href="../cards/rp-VCN30/"><code>rp-VCN30</code></a></dd></dl></div></details>
